"""A local page for trying the pipeline by hand.

    python src/app.py            then open http://localhost:8765

A relation map (a node and the nodes live edges tie it to, a capped list per relation,
click to move on),
then four boxes: type an intervention string the way a registry would write it and see
how the resolver reads it, or ask a question and see the grounded answer with its
evidence, give a structured answer (claims citing evidence edges) and see which
claims the graph supports and the answer written from them, or paste free prose and
see what the auxiliary word-order check flags. All of them call
the functions the pipeline uses (resolve_drug_string, OncoKG.answer,
OncoKG.check_groundedness) on the built artefacts, so what the page shows is what the
pipeline would do. Nothing is written; the language model is never called.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import sqlite3
import sys
import threading
import unicodedata
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import config as C
import normalize as N
import textrules as T
from query import OncoKG

PAGE = C.WEB / "try.html"
PORT = 8765


class Engine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.kg = OncoKG()
        # A browser opens spare connections it may never use, which would stall a
        # single-threaded server; requests are threaded, the engine is not.
        self.kg.conn.close()
        self.kg.conn = sqlite3.connect(C.PROCESSED / "oncokg.sqlite", check_same_thread=False)
        self.kg.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.dic = self.kg.dic
        self.index_keys = self.kg.index_keys
        target_rows, _, _ = N._load("targets")
        self.target_keys = N.target_guard_keys(target_rows, self.dic)
        # The names the relation map reads, under one rule (`_sig`). A drug is read only by
        # its ChEMBL preferred name. Every other stored name of a drug (trade names,
        # ChEMBL synonyms, synonyms copied from trial registrations) is where a product
        # gets filed under a molecule it is not: combination products under one
        # ingredient (Tobradex under dexamethasone), formulations (Myocet, LEP-ETU),
        # biosimilars under the reference molecule (Inflectra, CT-P13), and wrong
        # substances (bupivacaine hydrochloride under antimony). Those names are not read;
        # the reply asks for the preferred name. Every node's own label, target symbols,
        # and company and disease keys are read too.
        # Both tables take every stored spelling: the lookup index keeps one spelling per
        # drug and key, and a spelling it drops ("Cabozantinib (s)-malate" beside
        # "CABOZANTINIB S-MALATE") must still be checked for refusal.
        self.names: dict = {}
        self.alias_only: dict = {}
        for did, drug in self.dic["drugs"].items():
            _add_name(self.names, drug["name"], did)
            for a in drug.get("aliases") or []:
                if a.get("type") != "preferred_name":
                    _add_name(self.alias_only, a["alias"], did)
        for hits in self.dic["index"].values():
            for h in hits:
                if h.get("alias_type") != "preferred_name":
                    _add_name(self.alias_only, h["alias"], h["drug_id"])
        for r in self.kg.conn.execute("SELECT id, label FROM node"):
            _add_name(self.names, r["label"], r["id"])
        for t in self.kg.targets.values():
            _add_name(self.names, t["symbol"], t["target_id"])
        # names stored only as dictionary keys (letters and digits): company ids and
        # former names, the disease's synonyms
        self.keyed: dict = {}
        for k, ids in self.kg.companies.items():
            self.keyed.setdefault(k, []).extend(ids)
        for k in self.kg.disease["index"]:
            self.keyed.setdefault(k, []).append(self.kg.disease["disease_id"])

    def resolve(self, text: str) -> dict:
        out = N.resolve_drug_string(text, self.dic, self.index_keys, self.target_keys)
        for c in out["components"]:
            d = self.dic["drugs"].get(c.get("drug_id") or "")
            if d:
                c["drug"] = {k: d.get(k) for k in
                             ("name", "drug_type", "max_phase_overall", "max_phase_in_nsclc")}
                c["drug"]["mechanisms"] = [m.get("mechanismOfAction") if isinstance(m, dict) else m
                                           for m in (d.get("mechanisms") or [])][:3]
                c["drug"]["trials"] = self.kg.conn.execute(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM edge WHERE rel='EVALUATES' AND dst=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
                    (d["drug_id"],)).fetchone()[0]
                par = self.dic["drugs"].get(d.get("parent_molecule") or "")
                if par:
                    c["drug"]["parent"] = {"name": par["name"], "trials": self.kg.conn.execute(
                        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM edge WHERE rel='EVALUATES' AND dst=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
                        (par["drug_id"],)).fetchone()[0]}
            if c.get("candidates"):
                c["candidate_names"] = [
                    (self.dic["drugs"].get(x) or {}).get("name", x) for x in c["candidates"]]
        return out

    def explore(self, text: str, per_group: int = 30) -> dict:
        """One live node and the live nodes its live edges tie it to, grouped by relation.

        `text` is a node id or one stored name, compared under one rule (`_sig`: case,
        accents, spaces, punctuation and trademark signs aside; every other letter,
        digit and symbol must match; a name with a bracketed group that holds no letter
        or digit, such as the rotation sign in "(+/-)NICOTINE", is compared with its
        punctuation, as written): a drug's ChEMBL preferred name, a node's own
        label, a target symbol, or a company or disease key. Anything more (a second
        name, a qualifier, a payload, a dye, a sentence) matches no stored name and is
        not drawn; the reply says to enter the name alone. A drug's other stored names
        (trade names, synonyms, registry strings) are not read: they file combination
        products, formulations, biosimilars and wrong substances under a molecule; the
        reply asks for the preferred name. A name the question linker refuses (a
        biosimilar, a reformulation, a tracer) is refused, whether it is the input or the
        stored name the input matched. A name stored for several live nodes is not drawn
        either: they are listed and the reader picks one by id.

        Each group keeps `per_group` neighbours, sorted as the page states: trials by
        start date (latest first), drugs by trials in the graph, sponsors by trials they
        lead, disease targets by association score. Sponsorship keeps its role (lead
        sponsor or collaborator)."""
        conn = self.kg.conn
        matched = None
        ref = text.strip()
        if re.fullmatch(r"(?i)(nct|chembl|ensg)\d+", ref):
            ref = ref.upper()                         # an id typed in lower case
        row = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM node WHERE id=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
                           (ref,)).fetchone()
        if row:
            nid = row["id"]
        else:
            ents = self.kg.link_entities(text)
            refused = [e for e in ents if e["type"] == "Refused"]
            hits = [e for e in ents if e.get("id") and e["type"] != "Refused"]
            if refused:                       # any refused part: the input is not one plain name
                return {"refused": refused[0]["label"], "why": refused[0]["why"]}
            sig = _sig(text)
            found = dict(self.names.get(sig, {}))
            if sig and sig == T.canonical_key(text):          # nothing the key would drop
                for k in self.keyed.get(sig, []):
                    found.setdefault(k, None)
            ids = list(found)
            choices = []
            for oid in ids:
                lab = conn.execute("SELECT type, label FROM node WHERE id=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
                                   (oid,)).fetchone()
                if lab and all(c["id"] != oid for c in choices):
                    choices.append({"id": oid, "type": lab["type"], "label": lab["label"]})
            # a stored name the question linker refuses stays refused however the input
            # spells it ("Myocet" written "My/o/cet" or "Myocet\u2122")
            stored = [n for c in choices for n in found.get(c["id"]) or []]
            stored += [n for names in (self.alias_only.get(sig) or {}).values() for n in names]
            for name in stored:
                no = [e for e in self.kg.link_entities(name) if e["type"] == "Refused"]
                if no:
                    return {"refused": no[0]["label"], "why": no[0]["why"]}
            if not choices:
                if found:                     # stored, but its node is retired
                    return {"not_found": text}
                if self.alias_only.get(sig):  # a drug's trade name or synonym
                    return {"alias_only": text}
                return {"not_one_name": text} if hits else {"not_found": text}
            if len(choices) > 1:
                # one name, several nodes ("Roche"): none is drawn in place of the others;
                # the reader picks one by its id
                return {"choose": choices, "text": text}
            nid = choices[0]["id"]
            matched = (found.get(nid) or [None])[0]
        node = conn.execute("SELECT id, type, label, props FROM node WHERE id=? "
                            "AND retired_at IS NULL", (nid,)).fetchone()
        if node is None:                              # a name can outlive its node
            return {"not_found": text}
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT e.id AS eid, e.rel, e.src, e.props AS eprops, n.id, n.type, n.label, "
            "n.props AS nprops FROM edge e JOIN node n "
            "ON n.id = CASE WHEN e.src=? THEN e.dst ELSE e.src END "
            "WHERE (e.src=? OR e.dst=?) AND e.retired_at IS NULL AND n.retired_at IS NULL",
            (nid, nid, nid)).fetchall()
        drug_ids = list({r["id"] for r in rows if r["type"] == "Drug"} | {nid})
        trials = {}
        for i in range(0, len(drug_ids), 500):
            chunk = drug_ids[i:i + 500]
            trials.update(conn.execute(               # live trials only
                "SELECT e.dst, COUNT(*) FROM edge e JOIN node t ON t.id = e.src "
                "WHERE e.rel='EVALUATES' AND e.retired_at IS NULL AND t.retired_at IS NULL "
                f"AND e.dst IN ({','.join('?' * len(chunk))}) GROUP BY e.dst", chunk).fetchall())
        groups: dict = {}
        for r in rows:
            ep, np_ = _obj(r["eprops"]), _obj(r["nprops"])
            item = {"id": r["id"], "type": r["type"], "label": r["label"], "edge_id": r["eid"]}
            role = ep.get("role") if r["rel"] == "SPONSORS" else None
            if r["type"] == "Drug":
                item["trials"] = trials.get(r["id"], 0)
                item["max_phase"] = np_.get("max_phase_overall")
            if r["rel"] == "SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF":
                n = ep.get("evidence_trials")
                # a count the edge does not record stays unknown; 0 is only for sorting
                item["evidence_trials"] = n if _number(n) else None
                item["weight"] = n if _number(n) else 0
                item["sponsor_class"] = ep.get("sponsor_class")
            elif r["type"] == "Trial":
                item.update({k: np_.get(k) for k in ("phases", "status", "title", "start_date", "url")})
                item["weight"] = str(np_.get("start_date") or "")
            elif r["type"] == "Drug":
                item["weight"] = item["trials"]
            elif r["rel"] == "ASSOCIATED_WITH":
                score = ep.get("association_score")
                item["score"] = score if _number(score) else None
                item["weight"] = item["score"] or 0
            else:
                item["weight"] = 0
            if r["rel"] == "ACTS_ON":
                item["action"] = ep.get("action_type")
            if role:
                item["role"] = role
            key = (r["rel"], "out" if r["src"] == nid else "in", r["type"], role)
            groups.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
        out = []
        for (rel, direction, ntype, role), items in groups.items():
            items.sort(key=lambda x: x["label"] or "")              # names A to Z
            items.sort(key=lambda x: x["weight"], reverse=True)     # then by the stated weight
            out.append({"rel": rel, "dir": direction, "type": ntype, "role": role,
                        "total": len(items), "items": items[:per_group]})
        out.sort(key=lambda g: -g["total"])
        props = _obj(node["props"])
        return {"center": {"id": node["id"], "type": node["type"], "label": node["label"],
                           "matched_text": matched,
                           "trials": trials.get(node["id"], 0) if node["type"] == "Drug" else None,
                           "props": {k: props.get(k) for k in
                                     ("max_phase_overall", "drug_type", "phases", "status",
                                      "title", "url", "start_date") if props.get(k) is not None}},
                "groups": out}

    def ask(self, question: str) -> dict:
        return self.kg.answer(question, use_llm=False).to_dict()

    def structured(self, question: str, payload: str) -> dict:
        """Check a structured answer (claims citing edges) and write the answer from it."""
        kg = self.kg
        entities = kg.link_entities(question)
        evidence = kg.retrieve(kg.classify(question, entities), entities)
        res = kg.verify_structured(payload, evidence)
        res["evidence"] = [{"edge_id": e.edge_id, "statement": e.statement} for e in evidence]
        return res

    def check(self, question: str, answer: str) -> dict:
        """Check an answer someone else wrote against the evidence for the question."""
        kg = self.kg
        entities = kg.link_entities(question)
        intent = kg.classify(question, entities)
        evidence = kg.retrieve(intent, entities)
        checks = kg.check_groundedness(answer, evidence, entities, generated=True,
                                       vocabulary=set(self.dic["index"]),
                                       linker=kg.link_entities)
        return {"intent": intent, "entities": entities, "checks": checks,
                "evidence": [{"statement": e.statement, "source_record": e.source_record,
                              "source": e.source, "url": e.url} for e in evidence[:8]],
                "evidence_count": len(evidence)}


_MARKS = "®™©℠"          # registered, trade mark, copyright, service mark


def _sig(text: str) -> str:
    """The one rule by which the relation map compares a typed name with a stored one:
    trademark signs dropped before compatibility folding (so "™" never becomes the
    letters "TM"), then case and accents ignored and spaces, punctuation and invisible
    characters dropped. Letters of any script, digits and symbols are kept, so
    "trastu+zumab", "interferon" with a Greek gamma or "trastuzumab" with a radiation sign
    are not "trastuzumab" or "interferon".

    A name with a bracketed group that holds no letter or digit ("(+/-)NICOTINE",
    "(-)-ARTEMISININ") carries an optical-rotation descriptor, whose signs are
    punctuation. Such a name is compared character for character instead: only case,
    accents, spaces and invisible characters aside, and every dash read as "-". So
    "(-)-ARTEMISININ", "(-_)-ARTEMISININ" and "(+/-\\u2622)NICOTINE" match no stored name but
    their own; "(±)" and "(+/-)" are not taken as the same."""
    t = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", "".join(c for c in text or "" if c not in _MARKS))
    t = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", t.casefold())
    t = "".join(c for c in t if unicodedata.category(c)[0] not in "MC")
    if any(not any(unicodedata.category(c)[0] in "LN" for c in g) for g in _GROUP.findall(t)):
        return "".join("-" if c in _DASH else c for c in t if unicodedata.category(c)[0] != "Z")
    return "".join(c for c in t if unicodedata.category(c)[0] not in "PZ")


# Brackets of every kind (opening, closing and quotation punctuation) and every dash
_OPEN = "".join(chr(c) for c in range(0x110000) if unicodedata.category(chr(c)) in ("Ps", "Pi"))
_CLOSE = "".join(chr(c) for c in range(0x110000) if unicodedata.category(chr(c)) in ("Pe", "Pf"))
_GROUP = re.compile("[" + re.escape(_OPEN) + "]([^" + re.escape(_OPEN + _CLOSE) + "]*)["
                    + re.escape(_CLOSE) + "]")
_DASH = {chr(c) for c in range(0x110000) if unicodedata.category(chr(c)) == "Pd"} | {"−"}


def _add_name(into: dict, name: str, nid: str) -> None:
    if name and _sig(name):
        names = into.setdefault(_sig(name), {}).setdefault(nid, [])
        if name not in names:
            names.append(name)


def _number(v) -> bool:
    return isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) and v == v

"""Grounded question answering over the graph.

The retrieval step is the same resolver the pipeline uses, pointed at the
question instead of at a registry record. A question that names "Tagrisso" and a
graph that stores CHEMBL3353410 only meet if one vocabulary serves both, so the
dictionary is reused rather than reimplemented.

Three properties the answering layer holds to:

* **Every statement is traceable.** Each claim carries the edges it rests on,
  each edge carries its source record and fetch time.
* **The graph answers, not the model.** With a key, the language model is given
  the retrieved evidence and returns claims, not prose; each claim is checked
  against the graph and the answer is written from the accepted ones in the
  graph's own words (structured.py). With no key the evidence is rendered
  directly.
* **Nothing unsupported is shipped.** A claim the graph does not support is
  reported and left out. An unverifiable answer is worse than a short one.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import functools
import re
import sqlite3
import sys
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import config as C
import normalize as N
import textrules as T

DB_PATH = C.PROCESSED / "oncokg.sqlite"
TRIAL_URL = "https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/{}"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# evidence
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
@dataclass
class Evidence:
    statement: str
    edge_id: str
    relation: str
    source: str
    source_record: str | None
    fetched_at: str | None
    confidence: float
    tier: str | None = None
    url: str | None = None
    detail: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    def citation(self) -> str:
        base = self.source_record or self.source
        return f"[{base}]"


@dataclass
class Answer:
    question: str
    intent: str
    entities: list[dict]
    text: str
    evidence: list[Evidence]
    generated_by: str
    checks: dict

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "question": self.question,
            "intent": self.intent,
            "entities": self.entities,
            "answer": self.text,
            "generated_by": self.generated_by,
            "checks": self.checks,
            "evidence": [
                {"statement": e.statement, "relation": e.relation, "edge_id": e.edge_id,
                 "source": e.source, "source_record": e.source_record,
                 "fetched_at": e.fetched_at, "confidence": e.confidence,
                 "resolution_tier": e.tier, "url": e.url, **e.detail}
                for e in self.evidence
            ],
        }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# engine
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Words that list separately named things; a list item ends at one. "with" is not among
# them, because it also sits inside product descriptions ("paclitaxel with albumin bound
# nanoparticles").
_JOINERS = {"and", "or", "plus", "versus", "vs", "vs.", "+", "&", "then", "followed",
            "or/and", "and/or"}



# every Unicode dash (category Pd) and minus sign, read as a hyphen
_DASHES = "[" + "".join(re.escape(chr(c)) for c in range(0x110000)
                        if unicodedata.category(chr(c)) == "Pd" or chr(c) in "\u2212\u207b\u208b") + "]"


def _plain(text: str) -> str:
    """Accents off, as the alias key does."""
    t = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text or "")
    return unicodedata.normalize("NFC", "".join(c for c in t if not unicodedata.combining(c)))


def _alnum_words(text: str) -> list[str]:
    """Letters-and-digits words, with every other character a separator."""
    return re.findall(r"[A-Za-z0-9]+", _plain(text))


# everything that is not a letter or digit beside a name (brackets of any kind, signs,
# dashes, other punctuation, symbols, spaces, invisible characters): the run in which
# an optical-rotation descriptor would stand, and the signs themselves
_DESCRIPTOR_RUN = r"(?:[^\w]|_)*"
_SIGNS = re.compile(r"[+\-−±]")


def _named_alone(question: str, name: str) -> bool:
    """The name occurs as a word of its own with no optical-rotation descriptor beside
    it: "NICOTINE" and "(NICOTINE)", but not "(+/-)NICOTINE", "(+/-)(NICOTINE)",
    "((+/-))NICOTINE", "(+/-.)NICOTINE", "NICOTINE (+)" or a name glued to other letters
    or signs. Any sign in the run of non-letters next to the name counts, so a list
    joined by "+" or "-" ("NICOTINE + osimertinib") does not read it either."""
    for m in re.finditer(re.escape(name), question, re.I):
        before, after = question[:m.start()], question[m.end():]
        if re.search(r"[\w+\-/−±]$", before) or re.match(r"[\w+\-/−±]", after):
            continue                              # glued to letters or signs
        left = re.search(_DESCRIPTOR_RUN + "$", before).group(0)
        right = re.match(_DESCRIPTOR_RUN, after).group(0)
        if _SIGNS.search(left) or _SIGNS.search(right):
            continue                              # a descriptor beside it
        return True
    return False


def _fold(text: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(_DASHES, "-", unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text or ""))



# Mass numbers of the isotopes used in medicine, per element (lower case).
# Radioactive labels only: stable 2H, 13C and 15N mark chemistry ("13-cis"), not tracers.
_MEDICAL_MASS = {"h": {3}, "c": {11, 14}, "n": {13}, "o": {15}, "f": {18},
                 "p": {32}, "s": {35}, "cu": {61, 62, 64, 67}, "ga": {67, 68}, "br": {76, 77},
                 "rb": {82}, "sr": {89, 90}, "y": {86, 90}, "zr": {89}, "tc": {99}, "in": {111},
                 "i": {123, 124, 125, 131}, "sm": {153}, "lu": {177}, "re": {186, 188},
                 "pb": {203, 212}, "at": {211}, "ra": {223}, "ac": {225},
                 # therapy and imaging nuclides whose symbols do not start common words
                 "tb": {149, 152, 155, 161}, "th": {227}, "bi": {212, 213}, "sc": {43, 44, 47},
"""The try-it page calls the pipeline's own functions; these pin what it shows."""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "src"))
import config as C

if not (C.PROCESSED / "oncokg.sqlite").exists():
    pytest.skip("built graph missing - run src/run_all.py first", allow_module_level=True)

import app

ENGINE = app.Engine()


def tiers(text):
    return [c["tier"] for c in ENGINE.resolve(text)["components"]]


def _page(e, t):
    """What the page shows for t. The map draws a whole name itself. Longer input is not
    drawn (refused, or "enter one name"); the question boxes then read it with the linker,
    and that reading is returned in the map's shape so a test can check it."""
    r = e.explore(t)
    if "center" in r:
        return {**r, "also": []}
    assert r.get("refused") or r.get("not_one_name") or r.get("alias_only"), (t, r)
    hits = [x for x in e.kg.link_entities(t) if x.get("id") and x["type"] != "Refused"]
    assert hits, t
    return {"center": hits[0], "also": hits[1:], "linker": True}


def _linked(e, t):
    """The one entity the question linker reads in t, with nothing refused."""
    ents = e.kg.link_entities(t)
    assert not any(x["type"] == "Refused" for x in ents), (t, ents)
    hits = [x for x in ents if x.get("id")]
    assert len(hits) == 1, (t, ents)
    return hits[0]["id"]


def test_resolve_matches_pipeline_rules():
    assert tiers("placebo plus docetaxel") == ["BLOCKED_PLACEBO", "EXACT"]
    assert tiers("Mvasi") == ["BLOCKED_DISTINCT_PRODUCT"]
    assert tiers("EGFR") == ["BLOCKED_TARGET_TERM"]


def test_salt_record_reports_parent_molecule():
    drug = ENGINE.resolve("Tarceva")["components"][0]["drug"]
    assert drug["parent"]["name"] == "ERLOTINIB"


def test_check_flags_invented_name_and_number_not_relation():
    bad = ENGINE.check("Which drugs act on ALK?", "Alectinib and zorbatinib act on ALK.")
    assert {u["value"].lower() for u in bad["checks"]["unsupported_claims"]} == {"zorbatinib"}
    num = ENGINE.check("What trials evaluate Tagrisso?", "It is in 4,812 trials.")
    assert not num["checks"]["grounded"]
    # a false statement built from true tokens is caught by the claim check
    rel = ENGINE.check("Which companies are developing osimertinib?",
                       "AstraZeneca has discontinued osimertinib.")
    assert rel["checks"]["grounded"] is False


def test_explore_draws_a_drug_and_refuses_a_biosimilar():
    import app
    e = app.Engine()
    r = _page(e, "osimertinib")
    assert r["center"]["id"] == "CHEMBL3353410"
    rels = {(g["rel"], g["dir"]) for g in r["groups"]}
    assert ("ACTS_ON", "out") in rels and ("SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF", "in") in rels
    assert all(len(g["items"]) <= 30 for g in r["groups"])
    assert e.explore("Mvasi").get("refused")
    assert e.explore("no such thing zz").get("not_found")

+def request(path, text):

+        return {'route':path, 'request':body, 'status':r.status, 'response':json.load(r)}

The conclusion does not claim universal free-text refusal, correctness of resolver-owned brand lists, arbitrary stereochemical spelling equivalence, or complete scientific validation of upstream identities.

Axes remain separate: **Standards: zero hard violations. Spec: zero established map blockers; the three nonblocking findings above remain.**

    def _link(self, question: str) -> list[dict]:
        found: list[dict] = []
        seen: set = set()
        tokens: list[str] = []
        clause: list[int] = []          # which list piece each token is in
        inner: list[bool] = []          # token inside parentheses
        ci, depth = 0, 0
        for piece in re.split(r"[,;\uff0c\uff1b\u3001]", question):
            for raw in re.split(r"\s+", piece):
                # a slash or bar, glued or free, ends the piece ("bevacizumab - awwb/
                # osimertinib"); it must not hide a product suffix from the guards
                for j, part in enumerate(re.split(r"[/|]", raw)):
                    if j:
                        ci += 1
                    if not part:
                        continue
                    if re.search(r"\w", part):
                        # edge punctuation and wrappers off ("bevacizumab-awwb?",
                        # "(bevacizumab-awwb)"); an isotope ("[89Zr]") is checked as a word
                        t = re.sub(r"^[^\w]+|[^\w]+$", "", part)
                    elif part in ("-", "\u2013", "\u2014", "+", "&"):
                        t = part            # "bevacizumab - awwb" needs its dash
                    else:
                        t = ""
                    if t:
                        tokens.append(t)
                        clause.append(ci)
                        inner.append(depth > 0 or part.startswith("("))
                    depth = max(0, depth + part.count("(") - part.count(")"))
            ci += 1

        # The resolver's refusals apply to questions too: "Mvasi", "liposomal
        # doxorubicin" or "generic version of bevacizumab" must never answer as the
        # originator molecule. The text is cut into list items at commas, semicolons
        # and listing words ("and", "or", "plus", ...). An item that names a distinct
        # product or a tracer anywhere has every drug in it refused: which drug a
        # qualifier describes is not guessed, so "osimertinib with generic version of
        # bevacizumab" refuses both, while "osimertinib and generic version of
        # bevacizumab" keeps osimertinib. Targets and companies in the item still link.
        seg: list[int] = []
        n = 0
        for k, t in enumerate(tokens):
            # "and" inside parentheses describes one product ("doxorubicin
            # (nanoparticle and liposomal formulation)"); it does not list two
            if t.lower() in _JOINERS and not inner[k]:
                n += 1
                seg.append(-1)
                continue
            if k and clause[k] != clause[k - 1]:
                n += 1
            seg.append(n)
        refused: set[int] = {k for k, t in enumerate(tokens)
                             if T.is_distinct_product(t) or T.is_radiotracer(t)}
        drugs = self._drug_spans(tokens, {k for k in range(len(tokens)) if seg[k] < 0}, seg)
        dangling = None                 # a qualifier item with no drug, joined by "and"/"or"
        prev_end = -1
        for sid in sorted(set(seg) - {-1}):
            idx = [k for k in range(len(tokens)) if seg[k] == sid]
            text = " ".join(tokens[k] for k in idx)
            joined = (dangling is not None and idx[0] > prev_end + 1
                      and all(seg[k] < 0 for k in range(prev_end + 1, idx[0]))
                      and clause[idx[0]] == clause[prev_end])
            prev_end = idx[-1]
            hit = [k for a, b in drugs if seg[a] == sid for k in range(a, b)]
            if _qualified([tokens[k] for k in idx]):
                named = any(k in refused and (
                    T.canonical_key(tokens[k]) in self.dic["index"]
                    or tokens[k].lower() in T.BIOSIMILAR_BRANDS | T.REFORMULATION_NAMES)
                    for k in idx)
                if not hit and not named:
                    # "liposomal and nanoparticle formulation of doxorubicin": a bare
                    # qualifier carries over a listing word to the next item's drug; a
                    # product named by itself ("Mvasi and bevacizumab") does not
                    dangling = text
                    continue
                dangling = None
            elif joined and hit:
                text = f"{dangling} ... {text}"
                dangling = None
            else:
                dangling = None if not joined else dangling
                continue
            refused.update(hit)
            found.append({"type": "Refused", "id": None, "label": text,
                          "matched_text": text,
                          "why": "names a distinct product or tracer, not "
                                 "the originator molecule"})

        matched: set[int] = set()
        for size in range(min(5, len(tokens)), 0, -1):
            for i in range(len(tokens) - size + 1):
                if (refused | matched) & set(range(i, i + size)):
                    continue
                if size > 1 and (len(set(seg[i:i + size])) > 1 or min(seg[i:i + size]) < 0):
                    continue            # a name never spans two list items
                window = " ".join(tokens[i:i + size])
                if len(window) < 3:
                    continue
                key = T.canonical_key(window)

                hits = self.dic["index"].get(key)
                # Several words only make a drug name when the name itself has
                # several words. The key drops spaces, so "is a" read as the
                # abbreviation "isa" (isatuximab) in any question or answer.
                if hits and size > 1 and not re.search(r"[\s\-/]", hits[0]["alias"]):
                    hits = None
                if hits and len({h["drug_id"] for h in hits}) > 1:
                    # a key several drugs share is read only as the drug whose own
                    # preferred name it is, letter for letter, written as a word of its
                    # own: "NICOTINE", but not the "NICOTINE" left of "(+/-)NICOTINE"
                    own = [h for h in hits
                           if _fold(self.dic["drugs"][h["drug_id"]]["name"]).lower() == window.lower()]
                    if len({h["drug_id"] for h in own}) == 1 and _named_alone(question, window):
                        hits = own
                if hits and len({h["drug_id"] for h in hits}) == 1:
                    did = hits[0]["drug_id"]
                    why = self._marked(key, did, tokens, seg, i, i + size)
                    if why:
                        # the name carries a product suffix or sits beside an isotope:
                        # refuse it, never fall back to the bare molecule
                        found.append({"type": "Refused", "id": None, "label": why,
                                      "matched_text": why,
                                      "why": "names a distinct product or tracer, not "
                                             "the originator molecule"})
                        refused.update(range(i, i + size))
                        continue
                    if ("Drug", did) not in seen:
        start date (latest first), drugs by trials in the graph, sponsors by trials they
        lead, disease targets by association score. Sponsorship keeps its role (lead
        sponsor or collaborator)."""
        conn = self.kg.conn
        matched = None
        ref = text.strip()
        if re.fullmatch(r"(?i)(nct|chembl|ensg)\d+", ref):
            ref = ref.upper()                         # an id typed in lower case
        row = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM node WHERE id=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
                           (ref,)).fetchone()
        if row:
            nid = row["id"]
        else:
            ents = self.kg.link_entities(text)
            refused = [e for e in ents if e["type"] == "Refused"]
            hits = [e for e in ents if e.get("id") and e["type"] != "Refused"]
            if refused:                       # any refused part: the input is not one plain name
                return {"refused": refused[0]["label"], "why": refused[0]["why"]}
            sig = _sig(text)
            found = dict(self.names.get(sig, {}))
            if sig and sig == T.canonical_key(text):          # nothing the key would drop
                for k in self.keyed.get(sig, []):
                    found.setdefault(k, None)
            ids = list(found)
            choices = []
            for oid in ids:
                lab = conn.execute("SELECT type, label FROM node WHERE id=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
                                   (oid,)).fetchone()
                if lab and all(c["id"] != oid for c in choices):
                    choices.append({"id": oid, "type": lab["type"], "label": lab["label"]})
            # a stored name the question linker refuses stays refused however the input
            # spells it ("Myocet" written "My/o/cet" or "Myocet\u2122")
            stored = [n for c in choices for n in found.get(c["id"]) or []]
            stored += [n for names in (self.alias_only.get(sig) or {}).values() for n in names]
            for name in stored:
                no = [e for e in self.kg.link_entities(name) if e["type"] == "Refused"]
                if no:
                    return {"refused": no[0]["label"], "why": no[0]["why"]}
            if not choices:
                if found:                     # stored, but its node is retired
                    return {"not_found": text}
                if self.alias_only.get(sig):  # a drug's trade name or synonym
                    return {"alias_only": text}
                return {"not_one_name": text} if hits else {"not_found": text}
            if len(choices) > 1:
                # one name, several nodes ("Roche"): none is drawn in place of the others;
                # the reader picks one by its id
                return {"choose": choices, "text": text}
            nid = choices[0]["id"]
            matched = (found.get(nid) or [None])[0]
        node = conn.execute("SELECT id, type, label, props FROM node WHERE id=? "
                            "AND retired_at IS NULL", (nid,)).fetchone()
        if node is None:                              # a name can outlive its node
            return {"not_found": text}
        rows = conn.execute(
            "SELECT e.id AS eid, e.rel, e.src, e.props AS eprops, n.id, n.type, n.label, "
            "n.props AS nprops FROM edge e JOIN node n "
            "ON n.id = CASE WHEN e.src=? THEN e.dst ELSE e.src END "
            "WHERE (e.src=? OR e.dst=?) AND e.retired_at IS NULL AND n.retired_at IS NULL",
            (nid, nid, nid)).fetchall()
        drug_ids = list({r["id"] for r in rows if r["type"] == "Drug"} | {nid})
        trials = {}
        for i in range(0, len(drug_ids), 500):
            chunk = drug_ids[i:i + 500]
            trials.update(conn.execute(               # live trials only

    def _drug_spans(self, tokens: list[str], skip: set[int], seg: list[int]) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
        """Token spans that name exactly one drug, longest first, not overlapping, each
        inside one list item."""
        spans: list[tuple[int, int]] = []
        taken: set[int] = set(skip)
        for size in range(min(5, len(tokens)), 0, -1):
            for i in range(len(tokens) - size + 1):
                if taken & set(range(i, i + size)) or len(set(seg[i:i + size])) > 1:
                    continue
                window = " ".join(tokens[i:i + size])
                if len(window) < 3:
                    continue
                hits = self.dic["index"].get(T.canonical_key(window))
                if hits and size > 1 and not re.search(r"[\s\-/]", hits[0]["alias"]):
                    hits = None
                if hits and len({h["drug_id"] for h in hits}) == 1:
                    spans.append((i, i + size))
                    taken.update(range(i, i + size))
        return spans

    def org_family(self, company_id: str) -> list[str]:
        """The organisation plus any companies a curated parent edge links to it."""
        ids = {company_id}
        for row in self.conn.execute(
                "SELECT dst FROM edge WHERE rel='PARENT_OF' AND src=?"
                " AND retired_at IS NULL", (company_id,)):
            ids.add(row["dst"])
        return sorted(ids)

    # -- intent ------------------------------------------------------------ #

    # -- entity linking ---------------------------------------------------- #
    def link_entities(self, question: str) -> list[dict]:
        # one spelling for every check: compatibility forms folded, every dash a hyphen
        question = _fold(question)
        blocked = self._resolver_blocked(question)
        found = self._link(question)
        whole = self._whole_name(question, blocked, found)
        if whole:
            return [whole]
        if not blocked:
            return found
        # a drug the resolver refuses in this text is refused here too, whatever the
        # splitting below made of it (the two must agree before anything is linked)
        out = [e for e in found if not (e["type"] == "Drug" and e["id"] in blocked)]
        for text in sorted({t for t in blocked.values()}):
            if not any(e["type"] == "Refused" and (text in e["label"] or e["label"] in text)
                       for e in out):
                out.append({"type": "Refused", "id": None, "label": text, "matched_text": text,
                            "why": "names a distinct product or tracer, not the "
                                   "originator molecule"})
        return out

    def _resolver_blocked(self, text: str) -> dict:
        """Drug ids inside the pieces the intervention resolver refuses as a distinct
        product or a tracer, with the refused piece."""
        comps = []
        lone = set()
        pieces = {p.strip() for p in re.split(r"[,;\uff0c\uff1b\u3001/|]", text) if p.strip()}
        for piece in pieces:                         # each distinct list piece once
            words = [w for w in re.split(r"[^\w\-]+", piece) if w]
            if not self._drug_spans(words, set(), [0] * len(words)):
                continue                             # no drug in it: nothing to refuse
            got = self._resolve_cache.get(piece)
            if got is None:
                try:
                    got = N.resolve_drug_string(piece, self.dic, self.index_keys)["components"]
                except Exception:                    # never let a guard crash a lookup
                    got = []
                if len(self._resolve_cache) >= 4096:
                    self._resolve_cache.clear()
                self._resolve_cache[piece] = got
            if len(words) == 1 and T.canonical_key(piece) in self.dic["index"]:
                lone.add(piece)                      # one stored name on its own ("566C")
            comps += got
        out: dict = {}
        accepted = {c.get("drug_id") for c in comps
{

+import ast, hashlib, json, pathlib, subprocess, sys, time, types, urllib.request

+def save(name, value):
+    (OUT / name).write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')

+def request(path, text):
+    body = {'text': text}
+    if path == '/api/structured': body['answer'] = '{"claims":[]}'
+    if path == '/api/check': body['answer'] = 'NICOTINE is evaluated in trials.'
+    req = urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:8938' + path, json.dumps(body).encode(), {'Content-Type':'application/json'})

+        return {'route':path, 'request':body, 'status':r.status, 'response':json.load(r)}

# Spec axis

No blocking or should-fix specification violation established in `git diff 2b1aca1...HEAD` at `e821094d1544da4a133c53af24fedc569337d283`.

The round-38 requirement says the adjacent run is "every non-letter, non-digit character, so any sign in it blocks the reading." `src/query.py:120` now implements `(?:[^\w]|_)*`. An exhaustive check of all 1,114,112 Unicode codepoints against the independent `not str.isalnum()` predicate finds zero disagreements: 975,555 run characters and 138,557 boundaries. This establishes the character-class contract, not universal chemical interpretation of free text. The recorded sign-separated-list recall cost follows the requested rule.

The round-37 amendment requires a qualifying bracketed name to be "compared character for character" after its specified preprocessing. `src/app.py:111-114` and `web/try.html:54` now state the punctuation exception. Removing docstrings produces identical parent/current `app.py` ASTs; page inline scripts are also identical. No executable map or UI behavior was added beyond the shared-key question predicate.

The inherited name-policy requirement, "A refused stored name stays refused, however it is spelt," passes for all 182 literal refused source spellings across 13 tested forms. All 1,066 preferred names with each of six significant suffixes remain undrawn. The independent source oracle reconstructs allowed names from source records and SQL rather than `Engine.names`; its fresh 22,956-input sweep has zero membership, refusal, silent-ambiguity, choice-set failures or exceptions. Its compact map responses are unchanged from saved M37 results. This comparison does not rerun parent Engine code and omits relation groups, as did M37.

No scope creep or newly demonstrated source-identity defect. Existing source-data curation limitations are not claimed repaired by this code change. +import ast, hashlib, json, pathlib, subprocess, sys, time, types, urllib.request
+
+OUT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+import app, query
+
+def save(name, value):
+    (OUT / name).write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+
+def request(path, text):
+    body = {'text': text}
+    if path == '/api/structured': body['answer'] = '{"claims":[]}'
+    if path == '/api/check': body['answer'] = 'NICOTINE is evaluated in trials.'
+    req = urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:8938' + path, json.dumps(body).encode(), {'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=180) as r:
+        return {'route':path, 'request':body, 'status':r.status, 'response':json.load(r)}
+
+parent_source = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'show', '2b1aca1:src/query.py'], cwd=ROOT)
+(OUT/'parent_query.py').write_bytes(parent_source)
+prior = types.ModuleType('query_m38_parent')
+prior.__file__ = str(ROOT/'src/query.py')
+sys.modules[prior.__name__] = prior
+exec(compile(parent_source, prior.__file__, 'exec'), prior.__dict__)
+current = app.Engine()
+cls = app.OncoKG
+app.OncoKG = prior.OncoKG
+try: old = app.Engine()
+finally: app.OncoKG = cls
+
+def compact_map(result):
+    return {'state': next((k for k in ['center','refused','choose','alias_only','not_one_name','not_found','error'] if k in result), None),
+            'center': result.get('center', {}).get('id')}
+
+families = {}
+for file in ['question_punctuation_regression.json','question_punctuation_mirror.json']:

+    for row in rows: families[row['input']] = 'M37-S1-28-exact'
+for text in ['What trials evaluate \u3014+/-\u3015NICOTINE?', 'What trials evaluate NICOTINE\u00ab+/-\u00bb?',
+             'What trials evaluate (+/-)\u200bNICOTINE?']:
+    families[text] = 'M37-inherited-question-leaks'
+for text in ['What trials evaluate ((+/-))NICOTINE?', 'What does NICOTINE((+/-)) target?']:
+    families[text] = 'M36-S1'
+for text in ['What trials evaluate NICOTINE + osimertinib?', 'What trials evaluate osimertinib + NICOTINE?',

+started = time.perf_counter()
+rows = []
+for text, family in families.items():

+summary = []

+    calls = {x['route']:x['response'] for x in row['http']}
+    summary.append({'family':row['family'], 'input':row['input'],
+                    'parent_ids':[x.get('id') for x in row['parent_entities']],

+def explore_ast(text):
+    tree=ast.parse(text)
+    cls=next(x for x in tree.body if isinstance(x,ast.ClassDef) and x.name=='Engine')

+import ast, hashlib, json, pathlib, subprocess, sys, time, types, urllib.request
+
+OUT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT = OUT.parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+import app, query
+
+def save(name, value):
+    (OUT / name).write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+
+def request(path, text):
+    body = {'text': text}
+    if path == '/api/structured': body['answer'] = '{"claims":[]}'
+    if path == '/api/check': body['answer'] = 'NICOTINE is evaluated in trials.'
+    req = urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:8938' + path, json.dumps(body).encode(), {'Content-Type':'application/json'})
+    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=180) as r:
+        return {'route':path, 'request':body, 'status':r.status, 'response':json.load(r)}
+
+parent_source = subprocess.check_output(['git', 'show', '2b1aca1:src/query.py'], cwd=ROOT)
+(OUT/'parent_query.py').write_bytes(parent_source)
+prior = types.ModuleType('query_m38_parent')
+prior.__file__ = str(ROOT/'src/query.py')
+sys.modules[prior.__name__] = prior
+exec(compile(parent_source, prior.__file__, 'exec'), prior.__dict__)
+current = app.Engine()
+cls = app.OncoKG
+app.OncoKG = prior.OncoKG
+try: old = app.Engine()
+finally: app.OncoKG = cls
+
+def compact_map(result):
+    return {'state': next((k for k in ['center','refused','choose','alias_only','not_one_name','not_found','error'] if k in result), None),
+            'center': result.get('center', {}).get('id')}
+
+families = {}
+for file in ['question_punctuation_regression.json','question_punctuation_mirror.json']:
+    rows = json.loads((ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m37/contract'/file).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
+    for row in rows: families[row['input']] = 'M37-S1-28-exact'
+for text in ['What trials evaluate \u3014+/-\u3015NICOTINE?', 'What trials evaluate NICOTINE\u00ab+/-\u00bb?',
+             'What trials evaluate (+/-)\u200bNICOTINE?']:
+    families[text] = 'M37-inherited-question-leaks'
+for text in ['What trials evaluate ((+/-))NICOTINE?', 'What does NICOTINE((+/-)) target?']:
+    families[text] = 'M36-S1'
+for text in ['What trials evaluate NICOTINE + osimertinib?', 'What trials evaluate osimertinib + NICOTINE?',
+             'What trials evaluate NICOTINE - osimertinib?']:
+    families[text] = 'M37-S2-retained'
+for text in ['What trials evaluate NICOTINE?', 'What trials evaluate (NICOTINE)?',
+             'What trials evaluate NICOTINE, osimertinib?', 'What trials evaluate NICOTINE / osimertinib?',
+             'What trials evaluate NICOTINE and osimertinib?']:
+    families[text] = 'positive-question-controls'
+
+started = time.perf_counter()
+rows = []
+for text, family in families.items():
+    calls = [request(path, text) for path in ['/api/explore','/api/ask','/api/structured','/api/check']]
+    rows.append({'family':family, 'input':text, 'parent_entities':old.kg.link_entities(text),
+                 'current_entities':current.kg.link_entities(text), 'parent_ask':old.ask(text),
+                 'parent_map':old.explore(text), 'http':calls})
+save('exact_question_http.json', rows)
+summary = []
+for row in rows:
+    calls = {x['route']:x['response'] for x in row['http']}
+    summary.append({'family':row['family'], 'input':row['input'],
+                    'parent_ids':[x.get('id') for x in row['parent_entities']],
+                    'current_ids':[x.get('id') for x in row['current_entities']],
+                    'parent_evidence':len(row['parent_ask']['evidence']),
+                    'ask_evidence':len(calls['/api/ask']['evidence']),
+                    'structured_evidence':len(calls['/api/structured']['evidence']),
+                    'check_evidence':calls['/api/check']['evidence_count'],
+                    'map':compact_map(calls['/api/explore']),
+                    'parent_map':compact_map(row['parent_map'])})
+save('exact_question_summary.json', summary)
+failures=[]
+for row in summary:
+    if row['family'] in ['M37-S1-28-exact','M37-inherited-question-leaks','M36-S1']:
+        if row['current_ids'] or any(row[x] for x in ['ask_evidence','structured_evidence','check_evidence']): failures.append(row)
+    if row['map']['center']: failures.append(row)
+
+doc_inputs=[r['input'] for r in json.loads((ROOT/'audit_evidence_claims/m37/documentation_http.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))]
+doc_inputs += ['[+/-]NICOTINE', '( + / - )NICOTINE', '(+/\u2013)NICOTINE', '(+/-)nicotine',
+               '\uff08\uff0b/\uff0d\uff09NICOTINE', '(+/-)N\u00cdCOTINE']

+save('documentation_http.json',docs)

+(OUT/'served_try.html').write_bytes(page)
+parent_app = subprocess.check_output(['git','show','2b1aca1:src/app.py'],cwd=ROOT).decode('utf-8')
+current_app = (ROOT/'src/app.py').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+def explore_ast(text):
+    tree=ast.parse(text)
+    cls=next(x for x in tree.body if isinstance(x,ast.ClassDef) and x.name=='Engine')
+    f=next(x for x in cls.body if isinstance(x,ast.FunctionDef) and x.name=='explore')
+    f.body=f.body[1:]
+    return ast.dump(f,include_attributes=False)
+metadata={'head':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip(),
+          'parent':'2b1aca18faeef72a5e090dbc3af4ba6151b4ae80',

+save('root_confirmation.json',metadata)
+print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in metadata.items() if k not in ['docstring','page_section0_description']},ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)

+import hashlib, json, pathlib, re, socket, subprocess
+
+OUT=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+ROOT=OUT.parents[1]
+def read(name): return json.loads((OUT/name).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
+def save(name,obj): (OUT/name).write_text(json.dumps(obj,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
+def closed(port):
+    with socket.socket() as s:
+        s.settimeout(1)
+        return s.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1',port)) != 0
+
+ports={str(p):closed(p) for p in (8938,9800,9801)}
+server={'command':'python src/app.py 8938','owned_process':read('server_process.json'),
+        'readiness':'Actual GET / returned 200; 174 root POST response records retained.',
+        'shutdown':'Stopped observed python PID 25348 after history lane released port 8938.',
+        'discovery_note':'Get-NetTCPConnection was denied by CIM; read-only netstat identified the listener before Get-Process and Stop-Process.',
+        'ports_closed':ports}
+save('server_lifecycle.json',server)
+report=(OUT/'REPORT.md').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+links=re.findall(r'\[[^\]]*\]\(([^)]+)\)',report)
+missing=[p for p in links if not p.startswith(('http://','https://','#')) and not (OUT/p.split('#')[0]).exists()]
+integrity=read('integrity_final.json')
+source=read('source/validation.json')
+contract=read('contract/summary.json')
+root=read('root_confirmation.json')
+tests=read('pytest_meta.json')
+checks={
+ 'no_pending_or_draft':not re.search(r'PENDING|DRAFT',report),
+ 'ending_verdict_once':report.rstrip().endswith('VERDICT: APPROVE') and len(re.findall(r'^VERDICT:',report,re.M))==1,
+ 'links_exist':not missing,
+ 'all_source_validation_counts_zero':not any(source.values()),
+ 'all_contract_db_ui_assertions_pass':contract['db_errors']==0 and contract['db_assertions']['passed']==contract['db_assertions']['total']==19 and contract['ui_assertions']['passed']==contract['ui_assertions']['total']==26,
+ 'demos_and_shared_keys_unchanged':contract['demo_parent_changes']==contract['demo_http_mismatches']==contract['shared_key_corpus']['changes']==0,
+ 'exact_questions_pass':not root['question_assertion_failures'],
+ 'served_page_and_explore_ast_verified':root['served_page_matches_file'] and root['explore_ast_excluding_docstring_identical'],
+ 'pytest_366_passed':tests['exit_code']==0 and '366 passed' in (OUT/'pytest.txt').read_text(encoding='utf-8'),
+ 'tracked_data_hashes_unchanged':not integrity['changed'] and not integrity['added'] and not integrity['tracked_status'],
+ 'head_unchanged':subprocess.check_output(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT).decode().strip()==integrity['head']==root['head'],
+ 'current_tracked_diff_empty':subprocess.run(['git','diff','--quiet'],cwd=ROOT).returncode==0,
+ 'owned_ports_closed':all(ports.values()),
+}
+result={'checks':checks,'failed':[k for k,v in checks.items() if not v], 'missing_links':missing,
+        'local_links_checked':len(links),'report_sha256':hashlib.sha256((OUT/'REPORT.md').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+        'report_lines':len(report.splitlines()),'ports_closed':ports,'files_integrity_checked':integrity['files_checked']}
+save('report_validation.json',result)
+print(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
+assert not result['failed']
